gives that

(5.82)

\Tailp\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{\frac{r}{4}}(y_{0})}}{\left(\frac{r}{4}\right)^{\tau_{0}+s}};B_{\frac{r}{4}}(y_{0})\right)\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\Tailp\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{(2r)^{\tau_{0}+s}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right).

Similarly, (
2.10
) with

u=f
,
s=\frac{s}{p}

\rho=\frac{r}{4}
,
R=2r
,
\tau=\tau_{n_{\sigma}}
,
t=0
and
\alpha=\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}
, and Hölder’s inequality along with the fact that
p<q
imply that

(5.83)

\displaystyle\Tailp\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{\frac{r}{4}}(y_{0})}}{\left(\frac{r}{4}\right)^{\tau_{0}}};B_{\frac{r}{4}}(y_{0})\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle+c\Tailp\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{(2r)^{\tau_{0}}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right).

Combine (
5.81
), (
5.82
) and (
5.83
) together with the fact that
\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{4}}(y_{0})\subset\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})
to show that

(5.84)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+3}}(y_{0})}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Tailp\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{\left(2r\right)^{\tau_{0}+s}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right)\right)

\displaystyle+c\left(\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\Tails\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{\left(2r\right)^{\tau_{0}}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right)\right).

On the other hand, as in
5.25
, Lemma
4.2
with
\tilde{\gamma}=\gamma_{0}
,
\tau=\tau_{n_{\sigma}}
and
\alpha=\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}
yields that

(5.85)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\left[\sum_{d=1}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{n\sigma}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{P^{d}\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n\sigma}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\right],

whenever
\mathcal{Q}\equiv\mathcal{Q}_{\frac{r}{\sqrt{n}2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}}(z_{1},z_{2})\Subset\Omega\times\Omega
satisfying (
4.30
). Let us assume that
z_{1},z_{2}\in B_{r}(x_{0})
. We note that there is a constant
c=c(n,s,p,q,\sigma)
such that

(5.86)

\frac{r^{n+p\tau_{m}}}{c}\leq\mu_{\tau_{m}}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)\leq cr^{n+p\tau_{m}}\quad(m=0,1,\ldots,n_{\sigma})

by following the same lines for the proof of (
4.32
) along with the fact that

(5.87)

\frac{r}{\sqrt{n}2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}<|x-y|<2r\quad\text{for any }(x,y)\in\mathcal{Q}.

Therefore, using (
5.41
), (
5.86
), (
5.87
) and (
5.52
) , we have

(5.88)

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}.

Since
P^{d}\mathcal{Q}\Subset\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}}(z_{d})
, we estimate the the second term in the right-hand side of (
5.85
) as

c\left[\sum_{d=1}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{n\sigma}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{P^{d}\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n\sigma}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\right]\leq c\left[\sum_{d=1}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{n\sigma}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}}(z_{d})}|D^{\tau_{n\sigma}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}}}\right],

where we have applied Hölder’s inequality together with the fact that
p\leq\hat{q}
. In light of (
5.88
), the above inequality and (
5.84
) with
y_{0}=z_{d}
, we estimate the right-hand side of (
5.85
) as

(5.89)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Tailp\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{(2r)^{\tau_{0}+s}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right)\right]

\displaystyle+c\left[\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{0}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\Tailp\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{(2r)^{\tau_{0}}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right)\right].

Since
\mathcal{B}_{r}(x_{0})
is covered by finitely many balls
\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}}(y_{i})
and cubes
\mathcal{Q}_{\frac{r}{\sqrt{n}2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}}\left(z_{1,i},z_{2,i}\right)
satisfying (
4.30
) for
y_{i},z_{1,i},z_{2,i}\in B_{r}(x_{0})
,
(
1.8
) with
q=\hat{q}
follows by using the standard covering argument along with (
5.84
) and (
5.89
), and then using a few algebraic manipulations together with (
5.41
). Moreover, we have
D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u\in L^{\hat{q}}_{\mathrm{loc}}\left(\Omega\times\Omega\,;\,\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)
which follows from the combinations of (
5.81
) and (
5.85
) along with the fact that
D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u\in L^{p}_{\mathrm{loc}}\left(\Omega\times\Omega\,;\,\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)
. If
l_{q}=0
, then
\hat{q}=q
. Let
l_{q}>0
. Then
\hat{q}=p_{1}
. As in (
5.28
) and (
5.80
), we find that

(5.90)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+3}}(y_{0})}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}_{1}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+2}}(y_{0})}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{p_{1}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}+\Tailp\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+2}}(y_{0})}}{\left(\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+2}\right)^{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+\tau_{n_{\sigma}}+s}};B_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+2}}(y_{0})\right)\right)

\displaystyle+c\left(\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+2}}(y_{0})}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}}f|^{\hat{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}}}+\Tails\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+2}}(y_{0})}}{\left(\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+2}\right)^{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}};B_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+2}}(y_{0})\right)\right),

where
y_{0}\in B_{r}(x_{0})
and
\hat{q}_{1}
is defined in (
5.29
). Plugging (
5.81
) with
\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}
there, replaced by
\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}+2}}
into (
5.90
), and using (
2.10
) as in (
5.82
) and (
5.83
), we have

(5.91)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}}+3}}(y_{0})}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}_{1}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Tailp\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{\left(2r\right)^{\tau_{0}+s}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right)\right)

\displaystyle+c\left(\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\Tails\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{\left(2r\right)^{\tau_{0}}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right)\right).

On the contrary, as in (
5.25
), Lemma
4.2
with
\tilde{\gamma}=\gamma_{1}
,
p=p_{1}
,
\tau=\tau_{n_{\sigma}}
and
\alpha=\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}
gives that for
\mathcal{Q}\equiv\mathcal{Q}_{\frac{r}{\sqrt{n}2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}}(z_{1},z_{2})
satisfying (
4.30
),

(5.92)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}_{1}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\left[\sum_{d=1}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{n\sigma}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{P^{d}\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n\sigma}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{p_{1}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\right].

Let us assume
z_{1},z_{2}\in B_{r}(x_{0})
to see that

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}_{1}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\left[\sum_{d=1}^{2}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}}(z_{d})}|D^{\tau_{n\sigma}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{p_{1}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\right],

where we have used (
5.88
), (
5.52
) and the fact that
P_{d}\mathcal{Q}\subset\mathcal{B}_{\frac{r}{2^{n_{\sigma}+3}}}(z_{d})
. We now plug (
5.84
) with
\hat{q}=p_{1}
and
y_{0}=z_{d}
into the second term in the right-hand side of the above inequality, in order to find that

(5.93)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u|^{\hat{q}_{1}}\,d\mu_{\tau_{n\sigma}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\hat{q}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left[\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Tailp\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{(2r)^{\tau_{0}+s}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right)\right]

\displaystyle+c\left[\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{B}_{2r}(x_{0})}|D^{\tau_{0}}d_{0}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau_{0}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\Tailp\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}}{(2r)^{\tau_{0}}};B_{2r}(x_{0})\right)\right].

Taking into account (
5.91
) and (
5.93
), the standard covering argument and a few algebraic manipulation along with (
5.41
) give that (
1.8
) with
q=\hat{q}_{1}
. Furthermore, (
5.91
) and (
5.92
) along with the fact
D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u\in L^{p_{1}}_{\mathrm{loc}}\left(\Omega\times\Omega\,;\,\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)
imply that
D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u\in L^{\hat{q}}_{\mathrm{loc}}\left(\Omega\times\Omega\,;\,\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)
.
By iterating this procedure
l_{q}-1
times, we have (
1.8
) and
D^{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}d_{\alpha_{n_{\sigma}}+s}u\in L^{q}_{\mathrm{loc}}\left(\Omega\times\Omega\,;\,\mu_{\tau_{n_{\sigma}}}\right)
. Using (
5.42
), we complete that
u\in L^{q}_{\mathrm{loc}}\left(\Omega\times\Omega\,;\frac{\,dx\,dy}{|x-y|^{n+\sigma q}}\right)
. ∎
